f05ccd3b54969d36c12a6bb649f74c760bb3dabf|2026-10-25|Niko1221|DeepSeek engine: DS4_FAST_ATTN=1 runs the chunked prompt's packed-KV attention on the gfx11/gfx12 matrix cores (new sparse_attn_wmma_kernel, opt-in; the decode and verify paths stay on the scalar kernels). The matrix's rows are 16 heads of ONE query, so a block stages a 16-key tile once for all of them: Q (16 heads x 512) is the A operand of the scores, the key tile is B, and the same tile is B of the values' product; the scores' 64-dim slices are computed by the 8 warps and summed in shared, the values by a warp a 64-dim slice, the online softmax a head a thread.
